# Household hh_s32 — 32 days, Monday to Thursday

Seed-generated household. Times are clock times; ids are the receptacle and object ids used in events.jsonl. A line indented under an activity says where an object went when the activity ended and why. WHIM marks a placement that landed somewhere other than where the decision was heading. Text in [brackets] names the hidden cause the decision is attributed to.

## Household

Type: flatmates. Rooms: bathroom, bedroom_1, bedroom_2, bedroom_3, entry, kitchen, living, office.

Residents:

- **Leo** (resident_1): works from home; untidy (tidiness 0.37); punctual (jitter scale 0.84); rarely forgets pocket items; mood barely affects behaviour (sensitivity 0.34); sleeps in bedroom_1, works at the office desk. Hobbies: baking, journaling, music. Chores they take on: dusting, grocery_run, ironing, vacuuming.
- **Elena** (resident_2): retired; very tidy (tidiness 0.78); erratic timing (jitter scale 1.41); often forgets pocket items; mood barely affects behaviour (sensitivity 0.21); sleeps in bedroom_2, works at the bedroom_2 desk. Hobbies: baking, friends, photography. Chores they take on: ironing.
- **Aisha** (resident_3): works outside the home; very untidy (tidiness 0.25); punctual (jitter scale 0.78); sometimes forgets pocket items; mood strongly affects behaviour (sensitivity 0.78); sleeps in bedroom_3, works at the bedroom_3 desk. Hobbies: baking, photography. Chores they take on: dusting, fixing, grocery_run, ironing, vacuuming.

Pet: a dog, mainly looked after by Aisha.

## Day 8 — Tuesday

**Active causes**

- `ep:afternoon_slump:resident_1:d8`: Leo hits an afternoon slump: things get left where they were used for a couple of hours (14:04–15:45).
- `ep:craving_a_snack:resident_2:d8`: Elena is craving a snack and fits one in when there is a gap; the bowl and glass tend to be left out (14:50–15:20).
- `ep:on_a_roll:resident_1:d8`: Leo is on a roll with work: sessions run long, breaks are short, the mug stays on the desk (13:58–17:14).

Internal states (0–1): Leo energy 0.48, hurriedness 0.06, distraction 0.35; Elena energy 0.64, hurriedness 0.31, distraction 0.46; Aisha energy 0.49, hurriedness 0.69, distraction 0.62

**Active causes**

- `running_late:resident_3`: Aisha is running late all day (0.79): rushed putdowns, things dumped at the door, more whim.

## Day 13 — Sunday

**Active causes**

- `ep:craving_a_snack:resident_2:d13`: Elena is craving a snack and fits one in when there is a gap; the bowl and glass tend to be left out (15:07–15:37).
- `ep:long_call_with_a_friend:resident_1:d13`: Leo gets a long call from a friend and takes it on the couch; the phone is left there afterwards (20:43–21:28).
- `ep:restless_evening:resident_3:d13`: Aisha can't settle this evening and wanders between rooms with things in hand (20:12–24:00).

Internal states (0–1): Leo energy 0.71, hurriedness 0.28, distraction 0.46; Elena energy 0.73, hurriedness 0.62, distraction 0.41; Aisha energy 0.69, hurriedness 0.64, distraction 0.51

## Day 19 — Saturday

**Active causes**

- `ep:tidy_mood:resident_1:d19`: Leo is in a tidying mood for a while: a quick tidy of the living room, and things go back where they belong (11:53–13:53).
- `unwell_spell:resident_1` (Leo): Unwell for the day: resting, dosing, drinking more, not working and not going out. no work and no trips out; the day is spent between the bed, the sofa and a chair by the window, and a different thing is left behind in each.
- `low_energy:resident_1`: Leo has low energy (0.00): leaves things where they were used instead of putting them back.
